2026-08-14 · IA-T6 · 58e82f7 · merged to main · **Mailgun delivery.** `helpers/mailer.js` (global `fetch`, **no new dependency** — Node 20.20.2), no-op returning `skipped:'not_configured'` when any of the three env vars is absent, `AbortSignal.timeout(10s)`, fixed failure vocabulary (`invalid_recipient|timeout|network|HTTP <status>`) — never Mailgun's own text — and a `redact()` layer on both log sites. ⚠ Send happens strictly AFTER the transaction commits: IA-T3's invariants (bcrypt/loops outside, exactly two writes inside) are intact, and network I/O in a synchronous better-sqlite3 transaction would hold the write lock across a round trip. ⚠ A mail failure NEVER fails the approval — reviewer proved the timeout path live: 201 after 10086 ms, friend created, temp-password login works, fake key in neither body nor log.
⚠⚠ **THE MAJOR REVIEW FINDING — the feature would have shipped SILENTLY DEAD.** `deploy.sh` ran `pm2 restart $PM2_APP`, which relaunches from PM2's STORED per-process config and does **not** re-read `ecosystem.config.cjs`; the `pm2 start … --only` fallback only fires when restart fails, i.e. never for a running app. So `node_args` never reached the interpreter, `/var/www/gorifi/.env` was never loaded, the mailer stayed a no-op — and `not_configured` is the ONE outcome the dialog renders as **nothing**, so there was zero signal on any surface. **Measured on the live box before the fix:** `pm2 describe gorifi-staging` → `interpreter args │ N/A`, `pm2 jlist` → `node_args: []`. Fixed by restarting FROM THE FILE (`pm2 restart $REMOTE_PATH/ecosystem.config.cjs --only $PM2_APP`, fallback kept), reproduced empirically on PM2 7.0.3 both ways. ⚠ `--env-file-if-exists`, never `--env-file` (which aborts startup on a missing file, bricking any host without the secrets). A **boot log line** now makes a botched env rollout visible: `[mailer] Mailgun enabled for <domain> via <baseUrl>` / `[mailer] disabled (no …) — outbound mail is a no-op`; the key is never printed, not even a prefix.
⚠ The signed credentials message has ONE home: the backend owns it (`helpers/credentials-message.js`), mails that exact string, and returns it as `credentials_message`; the dialog copies what the server sent, so mail and clipboard are byte-identical BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by two maintained literals. Consequence: the server needs the login URL → `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` → `Origin` **intersected with the shared allowlist** (`config/origins.js`, now the one home, imported by `index.js`) → `https://podpultovka.biz`. ⚠ The Origin is allowlisted, not merely shape-checked, because that URL goes into an email a THIRD PARTY receives from a DKIM-signed brand domain: the vector was already closed by `cors` rejecting foreign origins on the actual request (measured 500, not just preflight), but nothing asserted that cross-file coupling. Reviewer notes the negative half of the new test is vacuous today for that reason — it is written as a property that holds under both layers.
⚠ `invalid_recipient` moved BELOW the config check: order is presence → config → validity. Before, a malformed address on any deployment without Mailgun env (local dev, the e2e recipe) raised the red "sa nepodarilo odoslať" alert although nothing was attempted, contradicting "`not_configured` ⇒ nothing user-facing". The recipient-PRESENCE check stays first — that ordering IS load-bearing for `no_recipient`.
⚠ **MAIL SAFETY IN THE SUITE.** Approval now mails, and the suite is target-agnostic while the documented staging target HOLDS the secrets — so ~20 approving tests would have fired real sends to `@example.*`, hard-bouncing on the brand's only sending domain. `e2e/fixtures.js` is the one home: `TARGET_IS_LOCAL` (parsed **hostname**, never a substring — `https://localhost.evil.example` and `https://real.host/localhost` both read NON-local; an unparseable URL fails towards "do not send"), `fixtureEmail()` (no address off-local ⇒ `no_recipient`, transport untouched), `setMailSafeTarget()` for the stub harness, and `test.skip(!TARGET_IS_LOCAL, …)` where an assertion genuinely needs a real address. `guest-lead-capture.spec.js`'s single approving test takes the SKIP deliberately — two assertions there are load-bearing on the address (the dialog summary and `created.email`), and `fixtureEmail()` would force both to become conditional, weakening exactly what that retarget exists to preserve. ⚠ A `TEMP FORCED` literal `false` on `TARGET_IS_LOCAL` was caught in the tree mid-round (transient debug state, since reverted and replaced with the real derivation) — a hardcoded `false` there removes the send-path coverage on EVERY target with a green suite, so the constant now carries a "never hardcode this" warning naming both failure modes. Verified by the orchestrator: 0 skipped locally, 13 mail-bearing tests actually RAN.
Gate: `node --check` clean on 9 files, `bash -n` on deploy.sh; 92 targeted / 631 full suite, 0 failed. Review: revise → 1 major + 5 minors, ALL fixed (+ the residual). E2E: covered by the row's own specs (server-side transport — `page.route` cannot see it, so the spec spawns its own backend with a fake key against a local `node:http` stub, blanking ambient env first).
2026-08-07 · RD-DS-3 · `task/rd-ds-3` · [PR #43](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/43) · **Form primitives + snapTab + the money helper, still inert.** Four new files: `neo/NeoStepper.vue`, `neo/NeoCheckbox.vue`, `lib/snap-tab.js`, `lib/money.js`. NeoCheckbox reuses RD-DS-2's `NeoIcon name="check"` rather than inlining a second tick path (verified: one copy of `M20 6L9 17l-5-5` in `icons.js`, rendered at stroke-width 3.6). **No `max` in the stepper** — confirmed 250 consecutive "+" taps keep incrementing; stock ceilings stay in the views. ⚠ **Three deliberate deviations from `ui.jsx`/the spec letter, all reviewer-endorsed with measurements.** (1) **The stepper suppresses the emit when the value cannot change** (at `min`, or on a disabled button) where ui.jsx emits unconditionally: `modelValue` is identical either way, but CLAUDE.md's reactivity idiom is `cart.value = {...cart.value}`, so an emit on a no-op tap changes the cart object's identity, wakes the deep `watch`, and fires a spurious debounced PUT **plus an orange "unsaved changes" banner on an untouched cart**. ui.jsx has no auto-save, so its unconditional emit costs nothing there and costs something here. Measured 8 clicks → 6 emits. (2) **JS guards sit on top of `disabled`/`pointer-events:none` and are load-bearing, not belt-and-braces** — the reviewer measured that `dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click'))` **does** reach a listener on a `<button disabled>` (while `.click()` does not), and that a disabled `.cbox` computes `pointer-events:auto` because the stylesheet has no `.cbox` disabled rule, making the JS guard its **only** protection. A locked cycle must not be mutable by any path. (3) `fmtEur` is **fail-closed on non-finite input** (`0.00 EUR`, never `NaN EUR`), byte-identical to `v.toFixed(2) + ' EUR'` across 200 000 random finite doubles plus edge values — zero divergences. ⚠ **One deviation was REVERTED on review**: the checkbox briefly used `tabindex="-1"` when `disabled`, reasoning by analogy to `<button disabled>`. But this is a `<span role="checkbox" aria-disabled="true">` — the case where WAI-ARIA APG says to **keep** the element focusable, since `aria-disabled` means "present but unavailable" as opposed to native `disabled`'s "gone". Shipping both got the worst of each: the element announced "unavailable" only to someone who could already reach it. It bites module 05 directly, where `disabled` marks locked/pending hand-over rows and **the disabled state IS the information** — a keyboard/AT user would never have encountered those rows. Now `tabindex="0"` (UC-DS-009's literal text) with the JS guards keeping it inert; verified a disabled+checked box takes a tab stop, reports `aria-checked="true"`, and still emits 0 on click/`.click()`/`dispatchEvent`/Space/Enter. `snapTab` is character-for-character UC-DS-012's body; proven by A/B rather than asserted — `snapTab` left `window.scrollY` at 0 while `scrollIntoView()` on the same page yanked it to 1441. `fmtEur` went in a **new `lib/money.js`** rather than reusing `guest-cart.js`'s identical-output `formatPrice`, because that module's charter is guest-cart primitives and importing it for a friend balance card would silently promote it to a shared money module; `money.js` carries an in-repo pointer naming the duplicate and RD-GX-1 as its resolver. Reviewer verdict **approve**, one round, three minors (two applied, one handed to RD-GX-1 — see that row). Gate re-run by the orchestrator after the fixes: `vite build` green, e2e **238 passed / 3 skipped**, zero spec edits; no new e2e spec by design (UC-DS-014 item 6 defers stepper/snapTab smoke to RD-FO-1/2). ⚠ **Operator note — a mid-run 236/3 was an orchestrator error, not a regression**: a server from the previous gate run still held :3997, so the new one died `EADDRINUSE` and the suite ran against a process whose `backend/public` had been `rm -rf`'d and rebuilt underneath it. That is exactly the `e2e/README.md` gotcha ("start a fresh server"); killing it and re-running gave a clean 238/3. Always confirm the port is free before seeding. Two things modules 04/06 must not trip over: `min > modelValue` makes a "−" tap **increase** the value (`Math.max(2,0)` → 2 — verbatim ui.jsx, so don't pass a `min` above the current quantity when clamping a cart down), and `modelValue` is `required` with no coercion, so a hand-rolled `cart[key]` binding that can be `undefined` renders an empty `.val` and makes "+" emit `NaN` (`guest-cart.js quantityIn()` already returns `|| 0`). Also unresolved for module 04: the prototype itself uses `.mono` for a body string (`<div class="mono">slivka, para orech, karamel</div>`), contradicting UC-DS-012's "mono is never used for running body text".

2026-08-07 · RD-DS-4 · `task/rd-ds-4` · [PR #44](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/44) · **Overlay primitives — module 02 is now COMPLETE and still inert.** `neo/NeoModal.vue` + `neo/NeoCopyRow.vue`. Plain `<Teleport to="body">`, **not radix** (`ui/dialog` stays admin-only per UC-DS-004 rule 4) — verified the layer's parent is `<body>`, `closest('.app')` is null, and the tokens still resolve there: `.btn.accent` inside the modal computes `rgb(255,45,135)`. That is the whole reason RD-DS-1's token block names `.app, .modal-layer`; **never optimise the teleport away**. ⚠ **Scroll-lock restore is COUNTED, not blanked** — a module-scope `openLocks` + `savedBodyOverflow` pair: only the *first* lock records the baseline (an inner one would record the `hidden` we just wrote and restore it forever) and only the *last* unlock restores. Pinned from both sides: `body.style.overflow='scroll'` → `hidden` → back to `'scroll'` (a naive `= ''` gives `''`), and an overlapping second modal's unmount leaves the page **still locked**. Survives a same-tick A-off/B-on swap and 20 rapid cycles. A per-instance `didLock` flag was added on review so lock/unlock cannot desynchronise even if `onBeforeUnmount` ever runs without `onMounted` (Vue skips a queued `mounted` on an already-unmounted instance while `bum` hooks run unguarded — real in principle, undrivable in practice, worst case one spurious unlock). ⚠ **All three close paths route through ONE JS guard**, per RD-DS-3's lesson that CSS and the `disabled` attribute don't stop `dispatchEvent` — verified `closable:false` is dead against a real scrim click, `scrim.click()`, a synthetic bubbling `MouseEvent` from a deep child, `keyboard.press('Escape')` and a synthetic `KeyboardEvent`; and that flipping `closable` false→true live re-enables Esc, so the flag is read at event time rather than captured. Focus enters the modal, returns to the opener (captured in `setup()`, restored *before* the node leaves the DOM — removing a focused element would reset `activeElement` to `<body>`), guards on `opener.isConnected`, and leaves no ring (`outline-style:none`). Unmount-while-open releases listener + lock + focus **without `@close` ever firing** — the parent-gated `v-if` model (UC-DS-010 lists no `open` prop) makes a mid-flight route change safe by construction. `NeoCopyRow` flips for 2000 ms (measured 2013 ms), **restarts** the timer on re-click rather than leaking it like the prototype, and flips **even when `writeText` rejects, throws synchronously, or `navigator.clipboard` is undefined** — a clipboard failure must never strand the UI. Copies the exact untruncated value while `.val` ellipsizes. `type="button"` added (the prototype omits it) so a copy row inside module 03's invite `<form>` can't submit it. ⚠ On review `NeoModal` gained **`v-bind="$attrs"` on `.modal`, bound FIRST**: `mergeProps` merges `class`/`style` and chains handlers but is **last-wins for everything else**, so attrs-first means a consumer's `class` composes with `.modal` while a stray `role`/`aria-modal`/`tabindex` **cannot clobber** the dialog semantics RD-KG-2's race-guard spec depends on. Without this a consumer's `data-testid` vanished silently (Vue drops it either way — `inheritAttrs` only changes whether you get warned). Reviewer verdict **approve**, one round, three minors (two applied, two findings raised as seams). Gate re-run by the orchestrator after the fixes: `vite build` green, e2e **238 passed / 3 skipped**, zero spec edits; no new e2e spec by design (UC-DS-014 item 6 defers modal/copy-row smoke to RD-FL-2/RD-FL-7). ⚠ **Two seams raised, neither patched here:** the **focus trap** (recorded as a blocker on RD-FL-2 — porting the radix forced-password gate to a trap-less `NeoModal` would make Tab a gate bypass) and the **scrim drag-close** (recorded as an open spec amendment above — needs a UC-DS-010 change, affects every form-bearing modal). Harness traps worth reusing: `overflow:hidden` on `<body>` does **not** block programmatic `window.scrollBy` (only `overflow:clip` does) — drive a real `page.mouse.wheel`; and the prototype must be served over HTTP, since `file://` makes Babel's XHR of the `.jsx` files fail CORS.

2026-08-07 · RD-FL-1 · `task/rd-fl-1` · [PR #45](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/45) · **The design system switches ON — first row that changes a rendered pixel.** `FriendPortal.vue` only (+63/−33): `class="app"` on the root, `BrandChrome` in all three states, loading/error restyle, page column moved to `mx-auto w-full max-w-[760px] px-4 sm:px-7`. **Templates only** — the extracted `<script setup>` block diffs `14a15`, i.e. one added import and nothing else, so every named function, computed, the `gorifi_friend_auth` shape, the `expiresAt` check, `window.scrollTo(0,0)` and the fire-and-forget hydrate are byte-identical. Legacy login (UC-FL-003) is untouched — the diff doesn't reach lines 864–986 at all — which matters because **the seed provisions legacy mode, so that's the branch the suite actually exercises**. Admin invariance proved by MD5: `/admin` login, dashboard and friends are byte-identical full-page PNGs against a build of the parent commit, with no class-set differences on any element. ⚠⚠ **THE FINDING OF THIS ROW, now in CLAUDE.md as its own section: `.app>*{position:relative;z-index:1}` neutralises EVERY Tailwind positioning utility on a direct child** — `fixed`, `absolute`, `sticky`, even `relative z-10` (z-index silently clamped to 1). Same `(0,1,0)` specificity, and `friends-theme.css` is imported after `style.css`, so it wins; the theme's own `.cartbar`/`.cat-tabs`/`.modal-layer` survive only because they sit *later in the same stylesheet*. Nested one level, the utilities are fine. **It fails silently — no build error, no failing spec.** The voucher overlay is a hand-rolled `div.fixed.inset-0.z-50` that *was* a direct child and would have collapsed into page flow, breaking UC-FL-001's "visually untouched" pin; fixed with `<Teleport to="body">`. The reviewer reproduced the hazard live and confirmed the teleport is not merely *a* fix but the *right* one: the counterfactual (wrap in a plain div, stay inside `.app`) restores `fixed` but makes the modal inherit `var(--font-body)` — measured `Figtree` vs today's `system-ui` — so "render inside `.app`" taken literally breaks the very requirement it accompanies. Every remaining screen row inherits this: a hand-rolled fixed overlay under `.app` must **teleport out** (when its subtree uses no theme tokens) or be **rebuilt on `NeoModal`/`.modal-layer`** (when it wants them). Radix-portaled dialogs and `NeoModal` are immune. Interim authenticated appbar: `#titles` holds plain `.t`/`.s` (name/uid) and `#trailing` carries the three existing shadcn ghost controls **verbatim** — same SVGs, `title` attrs and handlers — so nothing is lost and none of RD-FL-3's four owned elements (`chip acc`, `NeoIcon` glyphs, the `.titles` click handler, the pencil between `.titles` and `.grow`) is pre-empted. Reviewer verdict **approve**, one round, three minors: the landmine documentation (done here, in CLAUDE.md) plus two residuals handed to RD-FL-3 (see that row) — the 68 px banner overhang and the fact that `error` currently surfaces nowhere for an authenticated friend. A partially-completed review is not a review — don't salvage one.

2026-08-07 · RD-FL-2 · `task/rd-fl-2` · [PR #46](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/46) · **Modern login + the forced-password gate on NeoModal + the effort's FIRST e2e additions.** Suite goes **238 → 250 passed / 3 skipped** (12 new in `e2e/tests/modern-login.spec.js`), zero existing specs edited. ⚠⚠ **`h-screen` turned out to be a LIVE collision, not the latent one RD-DS-1's review concluded** — Tailwind's JIT emits a utility the moment its name appears in **scanned source**, so writing UC-FL-002's mandated `<h1 class="h-screen">` generated `.h-screen{height:100vh}` alongside `:where(.app,.modal-layer) .h-screen`: same specificity, **different property**, so BOTH applied and the login headline became a full viewport tall (measured 855 px gap at 378 px). Fixed centrally with `blocklist: ['h-screen']` in `tailwind.config.js` — the only instrument that scales, since renaming is impossible (the spec pins the class verbatim), inline `height:auto` would have to be repeated on every heading in 03–06 with one omission invisible until someone opens that screen, and a specificity bump can't win against a different property. `min-h-screen` is a **separate candidate** and still emitted (all 19 admin/guest views use it); the parent build never emitted the bare utility, so nothing pre-existing was removed. ⚠ **Consequence: bare `h-screen` outside `.app` now silently resolves to nothing** — use `min-h-screen` or `h-[100vh]`. ⚠ **THE BLOCKER IS CLOSED: `NeoModal` now has a focus trap** (`trapFocus: {type: Boolean, default: null}`, `trapping = trapFocus === null ? !closable : trapFocus`, so non-dismissable gates trap by default and ordinary dialogs are unchanged unless they opt in — `null` rather than `false` because Vue's Boolean casting must keep "absent" distinguishable from "explicitly off"). Tab is intercepted in the **existing** document `keydown` listener — no new listener, no new lifecycle — and the branch `return`s before the Esc path, so Esc semantics are untouched. Proven by temporary revert: without it, `Tab #3 escaped the gate → [...,"BODY"]`. ⚠ **The first implementation was REJECTED on review and rewritten**: it let the browser move focus and only intervened at the edges, which escaped four ways — a `<summary>` tail (focusable, missing from `FOCUSABLE`), an `[inert]` tail (counted by us, skipped by the browser), a `visibility:hidden` tail, and a **positive `tabindex`** reordering the real sequence. It also carried a **false comment** claiming `getClientRects()` covers `visibility:hidden` — it does not; such elements still generate boxes. `trapTab()` now **always** `preventDefault()`s and moves focus itself by index (`items[(idx ± 1 + n) % n]`), so containment no longer depends on the browser agreeing with our list; worst case is a wrong element *inside* the dialog. `focusFirstThatTakes()` is **load-bearing, and more so than its comment says**: without it a candidate that refuses focus parks focus on its predecessor forever (the next Tab recomputes the same index), and beyond `<details>` it is what skips a `<button>` inside `<fieldset disabled>` — which passes every filter yet Chromium refuses. It is hard-bounded (`step <= n`) and falls back to the container. Second-pass review re-ran all four vectors plus empty/single lists, focus-outside-on-arrival, 140 rapid presses, focus handlers that redirect, and an all-refusing list: **all contained**. This matters because `NeoModal` is the shell every 03–06 dialog composes on and `<details>` already ships in `FriendOrder.vue`/`GuestOrder.vue`/`GuestProductGrid.vue`. ⚠ **The new spec is HERMETIC — it must stay the idiom for every future modern-mode spec.** The first version flipped global `auth_mode` in `beforeAll`/`afterAll`; that was rejected because `fullyParallel: false` does **not** serialise *files* and no `workers` is pinned (so `cpus/2` — on a ≥4-core host a concurrent `public-flow`/`friend-login-list` sees the modern card and loses its `friend_sessions`, deleted once per flip), and a worker death before `afterAll` strands `auth_mode='modern'` so every later legacy spec fails misleadingly. Now a `beforeEach` stubs the one endpoint the view reads (`page.route('**/friends/auth-mode', …)`, which outlives navigation) and `afterAll` **asserts** the server still reports `legacy` rather than restoring it. Verified not vacuous: `auth_mode` appears nowhere in `backend/src/routes/friends.js`, so the personal-login branch is genuinely ungated — auth, the gate, `change-password` and the cycle list all hit the real backend. This also discharges 02 §UC-DS-014 item 6's deferred smoke for **BrandChrome, NeoIcon, NeoCheckbox and NeoModal** (`NeoCopyRow` stays RD-FL-6's). Legacy branch byte-identical — the reviewer diffed old 864–986 against new 978–1100 and got **empty output**; the modern branch is a new *preceding* `v-else-if`, and the gate is a standalone sibling, not spliced into a chain (the GSO-T6 trap). Admin invariance: five admin pages byte-for-byte identical PNGs against a parent-commit build **served on the same port** — a first pass on a different port showed `/admin/settings` differing by 99 bytes because that page renders `window.location.origin`. Reviewer verdict **approve** after one revise round. ⚠ **THEME-LEVEL FOLLOW-UP, schedule before RD-FL-3 lands:** every `.inp` is **52.5 px** vs the prototype's 48 and every `.btn` **47** vs 44 — Tailwind preflight's `button,input,…{line-height:inherit}` gives 1.5 where the prototype gets `normal` (~1.2). Arithmetic confirmed to the pixel by the reviewer. It is a systematic inflation on **every field and button in modules 03–06**, inherited from RD-DS-1's port, and until a one-rule amendment lands in `friends-theme.css` (`:where(.app,.modal-layer) .inp, … .btn { line-height: normal }`) every "match to the pixel" comparison starts from a known miss and each row re-litigates it. Left `forced-change-ui.spec.js` at `test.fixme` (un-fixme-ing would edit an existing spec, and its coverage is now redundant); whoever retires it must fix **two** things together — the stale `/resetoval vaše heslo/` regex (per UC-FL-012 the *regex* moves, not the copy) and `dialog.getByLabel('Nové heslo')`, now ambiguous under strict mode against "Potvrdiť nové heslo" (use `/^nové heslo$/i`, as the new spec already does).

2026-08-07 · RD-DS-5 · `task/rd-ds-5` · [PR #47](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/47) · **Two module-02 amendments module 03 was blocked on** — inserted as a row rather than done per-screen, because both touch **shared** design-system files and would otherwise be re-litigated in every remaining row. Suite **250 → 253 passed / 3 skipped** (`e2e/tests/neo-control-metrics.spec.js`, 3 tests), zero existing specs edited. **(A) `BrandChrome` gained `#after-titles` + a titles-click affordance**, closing the RD-FL-3 blocker: UC-DS-006 had declared the structure fixed at three slots, but `portal.jsx:97` puts `onClick` on the component-owned `.titles` div and `:101` puts the pencil *between* `.titles` and `.grow` — where `#trailing` (after the spacer) flings it to the right edge and `#titles` (a column flex) stacks it under the name. ⚠ **`titlesAction` is a label-carrying `String`, not a Boolean**: `.titles` holds the friend's name and code, so a bare `role="button"` announces "LEGO lego-4821, button" — the person's name, never the action's. Folding "is it interactive" and "what does it announce" into one prop makes an unlabelled interactive titles block **impossible to ship** rather than merely discouraged. `titlesClass` was rejected because a class can paint `cursor:pointer` but cannot attach a handler, so look and behaviour would become separable. Opt-in is **structural**: attributes *and* listeners are one `v-bind` object that is `{}` when inactive — a static `@click` would always register a handler even as a no-op, and that is what makes the no-regression proof possible. Verified independently by the reviewer across 8 prop/slot combinations plus a live-browser comparison: every element, attribute, class, text node and computed style identical, the **only** delta being the unused slot's fragment anchors (two empty text nodes, exactly what `#leading`/`#trailing` already emit), which form no anonymous flex item — `.titles` ends at 170, `.grow` starts at 184, one 14 px gap and nothing more. ⚠ The UC-DS-006 acceptance criterion was **corrected on review** to state that caveat: it first said "markup identical", which a strict whole-DOM comparison fails on the anchors while nothing is wrong — inviting a false regression report or a "fix" that removes the slot. **(B) Adaptation A9** — `:where(.app, .modal-layer) .inp, … .btn{line-height:normal}`. Tailwind preflight's `button,input,…{line-height:inherit}` gave 1.5 where the canon computes `normal`, inflating **every** field by 4.5 px and **every** button by 3 px. Now `.inp` 48 / `.btn` 44 / `.btn.sm` 38, matching the prototype exactly (reviewer re-derived the constants from the prototype served over HTTP, not from the build, so the assertions aren't tautological; deleting A9 from the CSSOM at runtime restores 52.5/47/41.5). ⚠ **Recorded as A9 in §UC-DS-001's exhaustive adaptation list** — the file is a byte-for-byte port and that section says "any other deviation is a fidelity bug", so an unrecorded addition reads as corruption to the next person diffing it. Scoped to the two **classes**, not to `button,input,…` inside `.app`: a broad element rule would restyle the shadcn children still rendering inside a half-migrated `.app` (UC-DS-002's transitional guarantee) — confirmed it would have hit `FriendPortal.vue:802-835`. `:where()`'s zero specificity leaves every compound variant's cascade untouched. Bare `.btn.ghost` renders **43** — not an invented number: the reviewer measured the canon computing exactly that (14px at `normal` + 20 padding + 6 border), and it clears UC-DS-005's 38 minimum. All hit-target minima still met; nothing had to be traded between the two specs.

2026-08-08 · RD-FL-3 · `task/rd-fl-3` · [PR #48](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/48) · **Authenticated appbar + balance card, and the first cross-session leak.** Suite **253 → 272 passed / 3 skipped** (`e2e/tests/portal-appbar.spec.js`, 19 tests), zero existing specs edited. The appbar is the first consumer of RD-DS-5's amendment: pencil in `#after-titles` (DOM order asserted — `.titles`→pencil→`.grow`, and geometrically `pencilLeft − titlesRight < 40px` while `chipLeft > pencilLeft + 40`, i.e. it hugs the name instead of being flung to the trailing edge, which was the whole point), `.titles` interactive via `titlesAction`. `FriendBalanceCard` stops importing `BalanceBadge` — which is **shared with admin** and stayed untouched, verified structurally (absent from the diff, and `FriendBalanceCard` is imported only by `FriendPortal`) *and* empirically (MD5-identical `/admin/friends`, `/admin/friends/1`, `/admin` against a parent-commit build **served on the same port**; `/admin/friends` renders `BalanceBadge` on 10 rows, so the comparison bites). ⚠⚠ **THE FINDING OF THIS ROW — `switchUser()` did not clear session-scoped display state, so one person's data rendered in the NEXT person's session.** The legacy dropdown lets anyone pick any friend, so on a shared device that is a *different human*. Reproduced end to end through the legacy dropdown with a different friend: fail A's profile save → banner → logout → log in as **B** → A's error banner still on screen. Three refs were affected and all three are now cleared: `error` (found first), plus `voucherResolved` — which **names money** ("Kredit 4.20 € pridaný") and whose own 5s timeout is set at *resolve* time, so it rendered on the **login screen** for whoever came next — and `subscriptions`, the worst of the three despite being invisible: `loadCycles`'s subscription GET is a **silent catch**, so if the next friend's GET fails the modal prefills the **previous** friend's preferences and "Uložiť" writes them onto the new account — a cross-account *write*, not a stale read. **The rule this row establishes: session-scoped display state dies with the session.** Every later row adding a ref that outlives a request must ask whether `switchUser()` should clear it. Note the pre-existing hazard was merely *invisible* before RD-FL-3 added the banner; surfacing it is what made clearing it this row's job. ⚠ **A second finding was an over-implementation of mine, corrected on review:** the pencil was given `role`/`tabindex`/`aria-label`, making it a **second button with the identical accessible name and action** adjacent to `.titles` — two consecutive "Upraviť profil, button" stops on a four-control bar, and a spec assertion of `toHaveCount(2)` that would have frozen the ambiguity into a contract. Not forced by either spec: §UC-FL-004 asks for `title` only and `portal.jsx:101` is a bare span, while §UC-DS-006's zero-pixel-ARIA clause is written about controls that are the **only** route to their action — the pencil is not. Now `aria-hidden="true"` + a testid, keeping `@click`/`title`; one tab stop, pointer behaviour unchanged. Also closed RD-FL-1's two residuals: the voucher banner moved onto the shared 760 px geometry, and the authenticated `error` banner now exists at all (previously **no** failure of `resolveVoucher`/`saveProfile`/`saveSubscriptions`/`openInviteModal` reached any UI). ⚠ But `resolveVoucher` is the one writer that banner can **never** serve — the voucher modal is `fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70` with no dismiss and stays open on failure, so the message sits behind the scrim; whichever row next touches that modal owns its in-modal error surface. Reviewer verdict **approve** after one revise round (two majors, both mutation-pinned) plus a second-pass minor applied here. Two OPENs remain open, used as recorded defaults and test-pinned: positive-balance green mono, and the transactions modal left as untouched shadcn. Notes for RD-FL-4..7: `getByRole('button', {name:'Upraviť profil'})` still collides with the profile modal's `DialogTitle`, so pick locators accordingly; the pencil (16 px) and logout (18 px) glyphs meet the prototype but **not** WCAG 2.5.8's 24 px, and RD-FL-4's gear has the same shape; `e2e/README.md`'s friends-list section was corrected (the dropdown reads the **public** `/friends/login-list`, restore hydrates via the **owner-gated** `/friends/:id/profile`, `api.getFriends()` has one caller left) — the `active=true` stub several specs still carry is **dead**, don't copy it forward.

All three pins verified live rather than argued: the `<h2>` accessible name concatenates across the `.hl` span; `cardFor()` resolves to exactly **1** for each of six active-card states and **0** for archive rows; the `<h3>`'s `textContent` strictly equals the cycle name with zero inner spans. ⚠ **Found while writing the tests: the balance card ALSO carries a literal `p-4`** (`p-4 sm:p-5`, from RD-FL-3). Harmless only because `cardFor()`'s `has:` filter excludes it (it contains no heading at all) — but it is why **the page column must never carry `p-4`**, which is now written into §UC-FL-006's Page-column paragraph where the next author reads it *before* writing markup. The archive rows deliberately use a plain bold `div` for the name so they cannot answer the locator. ⚠ **The backlog row for this task was WRONG and the conflict is recorded above** — "ships WITHOUT the share row" is unimplementable against an uneditable spec. ⚠ **A major caught on review: the plan block could scroll the whole page sideways.** `plan_note` is *free admin text*, so one pasted Google Sheets URL gave **531 px scrollWidth against a 320 px viewport — 211 px of horizontal overflow**, the exact production failure `mobile-no-h-overflow.spec.js` exists for. Fixed, and the fixture now *contains* an 80-char URL so the test actually exercises it: **320/320, zero overflow**, reproduced red-then-green. ⚠ **Second review finding — a spec that would have forced the next row to edit it:** the adjacency assertion pinned `lastChild.tagName === 'BUTTON'`, but UC-FL-007's share row is a **`div` wrapping** the button, so RD-FL-5's in-place replacement would have broken a spec RD-FL-4 had just created. Now structure-agnostic. ⚠ The implementer correctly **rejected the reviewer's literal fix** (`[aria-label="Zdieľať s kolegami"]`), which matches *neither* control: today's transitional button takes its name from visible text and has no `aria-label`, while UC-FL-007's future button has the `aria-label` but visible text just "Zdieľať" — the selector would have been vacuously false now and passed only after RD-FL-5. Matching by accessible name from **either** source is the fix. Behavioural nuance, reviewer-confirmed safe: money goes through `fmtEur`, so a submitted order totalling zero now renders `0.00 EUR` where `formatPrice` rendered `-` — strictly better, since `-` conflated "zero" with "absent" and both renders are gated on `hasOrder`, which the backend sets only for a submitted row. `showArchive` joins the session-scoped refs cleared by `switchUser()` (UC-FL-008 pins the fold default-closed, and a surviving component instance is the only path by which the next person lands on it open). Reviewer verdict **approve** after one revise round. Spec edits made here: §UC-FL-006 gains the `p-4` prohibition (written as "express horizontal padding with `px-*`; the all-sides `p-4` shorthand is banned; `py-6` is fine" — the brief's "horizontal padding only" would have been *false*, since the column does carry vertical padding), and §UC-FL-008's self-contradictory archive-badge parenthetical is replaced with what `portal.jsx:139` actually does (a plain `.badge` for **both** types). ⚠ **Operator note that costs a green-looking run:** `DB_PATH` must be exported to **both** the server *and* the Playwright process. Setting it only on the server makes the two direct-DB-write tests self-skip and the suite reads **288 passed / 5 skipped** — a *green* run two tests short, which is the dangerous shape of this mistake. Notes for RD-FL-6/7: `currentVoucher`/`pendingVouchers`/`showVoucherModal` still survive `switchUser()` (only `voucherResolved` is cleared) — unreachable today because the voucher scrim covers the logout control, but whichever row gives that modal a dismiss control makes it reachable. And the cycle card root is a bare `div` with `@click` and no keyboard route — identical to the pre-redesign shadcn `Card`, so no regression, and neither spec asks for one; flagged so it stays a decision rather than an oversight.

2026-08-08 · RD-FL-5 · `task/rd-fl-5` · [PR #50](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/50) · **Share row + colleague count — module 03's ONLY script change.** Suite **290 → 301 passed / 3 skipped** (`e2e/tests/portal-share-row.spec.js`, 11 tests), zero existing specs edited, `GuestShareDialog.vue` untouched. RD-FL-4's transitional shadcn button was **replaced in place**, and RD-FL-4's deliberately structure-agnostic adjacency assertion survived **without an edit** — the reviewer confirmed it is still meaningful rather than accidentally vacuous (it now matches via the `aria-label` branch on a descendant of the last child). ⚠ **Both guards on the new fetch were mutation-tested by the implementer AND independently re-mutated by the reviewer**, who confirmed they are **complementary, not redundant**: `guestCounts.value = {}` in `switchUser()` handles data that already landed, `++guestCountSeq` (bumped at the top of `loadCycles`, before any await) handles data still in flight — neither assumes the other. Deleting the seq guard fails exactly the two deferred tests (past-logout, past-second-`loadCycles`); deleting the clear fails exactly the no-reload logout test, which shows the **previous friend's count on screen**. The reviewer could construct neither a stale write nor a wrongly-dropped fresh response: the guard sits after the only `await` with no `await` between it and the write, and `loadCycles` can only throw before the batch fires. This matters more here than in RD-FL-3/4 — a stale value is **another friend's colleague data**, not merely stale UI. The fetch hangs off `loadCycles`'s tail rather than its four call sites, so the restore paths, both logins and the subscription refetch are all covered and the seq bump has one home. ⚠ **`guestCounts` is a MERGE map that is never reset per batch, so a refetch that FAILS leaves the previous count standing** rather than falling back to the "Objednávate aj pre kolegov?" copy. Reproduced by the reviewer (seed 5 → flip the stub to 500 → save subscriptions → badge still reads 5). Kept as-is — showing last-known-good is the better UX and it is **not** a cross-session leak, since `switchUser()` is the only authenticated→login transition (the other seven `authState='login'` sites are all in `onMounted`-only `loadInitialData` plus one dead branch) — but the in-code comment was corrected, because it claimed a self-healing that only holds on the success path. Zero counts are **written, not skipped** (a decrementing count must be able to clear without a logout), with `Number.isFinite` landing a malformed payload on 0 rather than `NaN`; the badge is suppressed at 0, matching `portal.jsx:76`'s `c.guestCount ? … : null`. ⚠ **`screenshots/02-shot.png` CANNOT verify this row — it crops mid-card immediately after the plan block** and never reaches the badge row or the share row. Verified against `friends/portal.jsx:73-81` and the live prototype over HTTP instead, reading computed values; the reviewer judged that superior to eyeballing a raster. The file's §Design-reference line now carries that caveat, and the test title no longer names the shot. Reviewer verdict **approve**, one round, two minors (both applied here). ⚠ **Seams for RD-KG-2:** `portal-share-row.spec.js:305-318` now pins from the **portal side** that the dialog has `role="dialog"`, **contains the shared cycle's name as text**, does **not** contain the other open cycle's name, closes on Escape to `toHaveCount(0)`, and re-targets as a single instance — moving the cycle name into a `title`/`aria-label`, truncating it, or adding a cycle picker breaks it. **For RD-FL-6..8:** `switchUser()` is currently the only session-ending path, which is what makes one clearing site sufficient; any new one (a global 401 handler — `api.js` has none today — or a session-expiry redirect) must clear `guestCounts` **and** bump `guestCountSeq`, since doing only one reopens the leak. The share row must stay the card's **last** child (two specs fail otherwise), and `data-testid="share-row"` is now load-bearing in six assertions. Recorded for the spec owner, not a defect here: **"N kolegovia" is grammatically wrong in Slovak for N≥5** ("kolegov"), but `portal.jsx:77` writes the single form and 01-architecture §i18n makes prototype copy final — inventing pluralization was correctly declined.

2026-08-08 · RD-FL-6 · `task/rd-fl-6` · [PR #51](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/51) · **Profile modal on `NeoModal`, and TWO plaintext-password leaks.** Suite **301 → 324 passed / 3 skipped** (`e2e/tests/portal-profile-modal.spec.js`, 23 tests), zero existing specs edited. Two revise rounds. ⚠⚠ **THE FINDING: friend B's credential-setup dialog auto-opened PRE-FILLED WITH FRIEND A'S PLAINTEXT PASSWORD.** `setupUsername`/`setupPassword`/`setupPasswordConfirm`/`setupError`/`showCredentialSetup` all survived `switchUser()`, and `authenticate()` **re-raises** `showCredentialSetup` for the next friend — so the dialog rendered itself, with **no action by B**, and "Nastaviť" would have written A's password onto B's account. Reproduced end to end (transition mode, one instance, no reload): `password field = "LEAKED-SECRET-1"`. Strictly worse than the `changeCurrentPassword` case this row set out to fix, which at least needed the next person to open a modal. Also cleared: `usernameAvailable`/`usernameChecking` **and `clearTimeout(usernameCheckTimeout)`** — `checkUsernameAvailability` has no guard at all, so its 400 ms callback lands *after* the clear and writes "Užívateľské meno je voľné" under B's empty field. A second, milder instance found on the next pass: **`showLoginPassword`** left the next person's own password field rendering as `type="text"` — it exposes no prior secret, but it inverts a security default across a session boundary on a shared device. ⚠ **This is the SIXTH consecutive row to find something surviving `switchUser()`, and the sixth miss was found by a reviewer's sweep, not by reviewing the ref list — so reviewing the list has demonstrably stopped working.** A four-item structural plan is now recorded above the RD-FL-8 row; items 2 and 3 go to RD-FL-7, item 4 (extract `FriendPortalSession.vue` with `:key="friendId"`, so logout destroys the instance and **there is no list to forget**) to RD-FL-8. A lint rule was explicitly rejected — no ESLint exists, and the rule still couldn't tell a session ref from a device preference. **Discharged RD-DS-4's open spec amendment**: the profile modal is the first *closable* form-bearing modal, so the scrim **drag-close** became live here. A press inside `.m-body` released over the scrim fires `click` on the nearest common ancestor — the scrim — so `@click.self` matched and the half-filled form was lost; instrumented and confirmed the pre-fix code *would* have closed. Now the mousedown origin must also be the scrim. ⚠ Three refinements the reviewer forced, all reproduced: the origin listener runs in the **capture phase** (a descendant's `stopPropagation` on `mousedown` otherwise leaves the flag latched and **re-opens the whole hazard** — this is the shared shell modules 04–06 fill with checkout, pickup, payment and third-party content); the flag is **primary-button-only** (a right- or middle-click sets it and produces **no `click`** to consume it, so a later programmatic click closed the modal); and `openProfileModal()` clears `error`, since a message left by another writer rendered inside the modal as if the profile save had failed, with no `Chyba:` prefix and no dismiss ×. ⚠ **A hypothesis was RETIRED with evidence rather than guarded against:** the reviewer suspected a classic scrollbar drag on `.modal-scrim` (`overflow-y:auto`) would close the modal, and neither party could reproduce it — because **Playwright launches headless Chromium with `--hide-scrollbars`**. With `ignoreDefaultArgs: ['--hide-scrollbars']` at 420×300 a real 15 px gutter appears, and thumb drag, track click, thumb click and arrow click each deliver `mousedown` + `mouseup` on the scrim and **no `click` at all**. An `offsetX` guard would have been dead code. Reviewer-endorsed judgement calls: **one scrim handler instead of the recorded two** (the two-handler form depends on listener registration order); **consume-on-close**; the **`label id` + `aria-labelledby` inversion** for the two read-only `div`s (`<label for>` only associates with labelable elements, so a plain `for` would have bound to nothing); and the page-banner **suppression** while the modal is open (the three non-voucher `error` writers each need their own modal open and the voucher overlay sits on top of everything, so two surfaces at once is unreachable). ⚠ Also fixed a genuine flake with a diagnosis worth keeping: the credential test passed alone and failed in a full run because `loadGuestCounts` `Promise.all`s one request per open cycle — **135** in a full-suite DB — so two logins in one page queue ~270 XHRs behind the browser's 6-connection limit and starve the second login's own calls ("Overujem…" forever). Muted in the two two-login tests; the underlying unbounded fan-out is handed to RD-FL-8, since cycles are never auto-closed and it degrades silently with age.

The reviewer prescribed `aria-label="Zavrieť dialóg"`; the implementer **measured that it still collides**, because Playwright matches `getByRole(…, {name})` as a whitespace-trimmed **case-insensitive SUBSTRING** unless `exact: true` — which those three specs don't pass. It failed 4 tests on the first gate run. ⚠ **The rule is therefore NON-CONTAINMENT, not inequality**, and since the three specs can't be edited to add `exact: true`, only a **synonym** works: **`aria-label="Zatvoriť dialóg"`**. Names now read `["Zatvoriť dialóg","Kopírovať","Zavrieť"]`, count 1. Recorded in §UC-DS-010 with the substring mechanism and the failed attempt, so nobody "simplifies" it back. That also let one of the three spec edits be **reverted**. ⚠ **On the "zero existing spec edits" property — it was self-contradictory and is now reformulated in `03 §UC-FL-013`.** `UC-FL-013` said "no spec files modified by this task" while `UC-FL-011` **in the same document** mandates moving the invite fetch error out of the page banner, invalidating an RD-FL-3 assertion. New rule: **pre-existing specs are immutable**; a *pipeline-authored* spec may be edited only when a module spec mandates the behaviour change or the assertion is structurally unsatisfiable against a mandated primitive — re-pointing, never weakening, with the clause cited and the count reported. Final count here: **1 file, 3 hunks**, both surviving edits forced (`input`/`toHaveValue` → `.copyrow .val`/`toHaveText`, since UC-DS-011 makes the value box a `div`; and the relocated invite error, which strictly *strengthens* — it now also asserts the page banner stays clean). **The session-boundary net** (`portal-session-boundary.spec.js`) enumerates the **surface, not the refs**, so it survives later rows adding state: walk the authenticated surface filling `SENTINEL-<n>`, log out, log B in, re-walk, assert no sentinel in `page.content()`, every password-ish field masked, every value `''`-or-B's-own, and — added on review — **every visible `[aria-pressed]` reads `"false"`** (catches the `showLoginPassword` class for *any* future reveal toggle without naming a ref, with an anti-vacuity guard since only the login screen carries one today). ⚠ **Its reach is honestly stated in its own header: of the six leaks that motivated it, it would have caught TWO.** It misses state that renders but is neither a field value nor a password type (`voucherResolved`, `showArchive`, `guestCounts`, and all of this row's own clears), and **a green run is NOT evidence for the `show*Modal` clears** — the scrim makes the logout control unclickable while a dialog is open, so those are by-construction defence this file cannot falsify. Bites verified three ways, each a rebuild: the implementer mutated `showLoginPassword` and `changeCurrentPassword`, the reviewer independently mutated `setupUsername`. Two more `switchUser()` residuals closed: **`setupSaving`** — worst of the class, because that dialog is **auto-raised** for the next person and both footer buttons are `:disabled="setupSaving"`, so B gets an inert dialog they did nothing to summon (reachable via "submit → Esc → logout"; Esc closes a `closable` dialog regardless of a disabled footer) — and `changePasswordSaving`. The implementer also cleared **`profileSaving`, which I had deferred**, arguing the deferral's basis was the *error-surface* convergence seam while a boolean in-flight flag raises no such question, and leaving one member of a class uncleared is an exception the next sweep must re-derive. **Accepted.** `inviteError` (a dedicated ref rather than the shared `error`) and `inviteSeq` (the `guestCountSeq` precedent — an invite code is an identity, and clearing the ref cannot stop a late response) both reviewer-approved. Note: `changePasswordSaving`/`profileSaving` are fixed but **not** test-covered — only `setupSaving`'s dialog auto-raises, so only it admits a test pinning real user-visible harm; RD-FL-8's extraction retires all three.

2026-08-08 · RD-FL-8a · `task/rd-fl-8a` · [PR #53](https://github.com/karolskolar/gorifi/pull/53) · **The `switchUser()` leak class is over, by construction.** `FriendPortal.vue` 2117 → 882 lines; the authenticated surface is now `FriendPortalSession.vue` (1412), mounted `v-if="authState === 'authenticated'"`. Net **+487 / −1526**. Suite **339 → 342 passed / 3 skipped**; **zero pre-existing specs touched** (four pipeline-authored files: two pure additions, two re-points with the mandate cited per §UC-FL-013). ⚠ **`switchUser()` shrank from ~35 assignments to 11**, and the reviewer walked the old block line by line to confirm nothing that should still be cleared was dropped. The survivors are the credential store, appbar identity and the login form — none reachable by an unmount. ⚠ **The extraction silently closed a SEVENTH leak class** the old `switchUser()` never cleared at all: `showVoucherModal`/`currentVoucher`/`pendingVouchers`, `shareCycle`, and the four forced-gate refs. The voucher one **rendered another friend's € amount over the login screen**. ⚠ **The `:key` story changed twice under measurement, and the second correction matters.** Removing `:key` while keeping `v-if` leaves the suite **green** — the unmount is what protects today. But `:key="friendId"` was **incompletely calibrated**: `entry` (the handshake payload: cycles, subscriptions, gate flags) is seeded into the child **once at setup** and produced *before* the child exists, while `authenticatePersonal` writes `selectedFriendId` and only *then* awaits `beginSession()`. So Vue flushes the key at the first `await` and a friend-switch-in-place would mount with the **previous friend's cycle list**, permanently, firing `loadGuestCounts` over A's cycle ids with B's token — **the key protects the refs and leaks the payload.** Now keyed on `sessionSeq`, bumped inside `beginSession()` one statement before `entry.value`, so it fires exactly when a fresh payload is ready and also covers the same friend re-authenticating. Proved in a standalone Vue page reproducing the sequence: identity-key → `{who:'A'}`, sessionSeq-key → `{who:'B'}`. ⚠ **`FriendPortalSession` is a FRAGMENT (9 root nodes), so `v-show` on it is a Vue no-op** — that fact is load-bearing (it is why `v-show` can never be the "cheap" refactor here) and is now stated in the file. ⚠ **A comment block asserting a measured `<KeepAlive>` matrix was REMOVED rather than repeated**: the implementer could not reproduce it (the only placement that keeps the `v-if` chain compiling puts `KeepAlive` *inside* the branch, where it caches nothing) and retracted their own earlier claim instead of standing on it. Replaced with the timing experiment, which proves the same point. **A10 grew from 3 classes to 10** on review — `.field-lbl`, `.field-help`, `.sub`, `.appbar .chip`, `.badge`, `.banner`, `.copyrow .val`, `.tab`, `.tbl td`, `.statuspill`. Stopping at three would have left **module 03's own shipped surface** inflated at the exact moment RD-FL-8b baselines it, and `.tab` is **module 04's sticky strip** — measured 45 px against the canon's 42 `min-height`, i.e. at 1.5 the content alone exceeded the declared minimum, so `min-height` had **stopped governing at all**. The port now reproduces the canon **exactly on all 17 probed fixtures**; every delta was a reduction; an unpredicted knock-on is that `.copyrow`'s copy button was being *stretched* by the inflated `.val` and returns to its canon 44. Excluded and named: `.tabbadge` (delta 0 — fixed `height` + `inline-flex` centring). ⚠ **Measurement trap worth keeping: `normal` is FONT-METRIC driven, and Google Fonts only downloads a family when something uses it** — a first probe read `.copyrow .val` 3 px high purely because Courier Prime had not swapped in. Force-load fonts on both sides before comparing. Also fixed: the guest-count worker queue kept **dispatching after logout** (~132 token-less 401s on the e2e DB) because `onBeforeUnmount` cancelled the username debounce but never bumped the sequence counters — the new concurrency cap was *masking* it, not fixing it. ⚠ **Two process errors the implementer flagged against themselves, both worth adopting:** piping `vite build` through `| tail -2 &&` takes **`tail`'s** exit status, so three "mutation" probes silently ran against a **stale bundle** and produced non-discriminating results; and dropping the `RATE_LIMIT_*` budgets on a server restart produced 29 unrelated failures, exactly as `e2e/README.md` warns. **The orchestrator had the same `| tail` habit and now checks build/suite exit codes explicitly.** Reviewer verdict **approve** after one revise round (1 major, 5 minors). Seams for RD-FL-8b and 04–06: the `entry` handoff pattern (parent probes, hands the payload down, child seeds once) **does not survive a mid-session identity change** — if module 04 adopts it for `FriendOrder`, key on the handshake; a fragment child silently no-ops any wrapper directive; and the page-level `error` banner is now single-writer and permanently behind the voucher scrim, so whichever row next touches that modal should give it an in-modal surface and retire the banner with its last writer. ⚠ **New house rule, now in the spec: no HTML comment may live in `BrandChrome.vue`'s template** — Vue keeps template comments in the DOM in dev builds (`comments` defaults to `__DEV__`), and the first cut's slot rationale rendered into the markup. It codifies existing practice: all six `neo/` primitives already carry zero template comments. Reviewer verdict **approve**, one round, one minor (the AC wording, fixed). Two notes for later: `neo-control-metrics.spec.js` awaits `document.fonts.ready` but the mono face loads lazily on first use, so the `inp mono` row measures against the fallback (48 vs the canon's 46) — harmless while it only asserts `line-height:normal` and `≥46`, but tightening it to `near(…, 46)` would flake; and `onTitlesKeydown` fires on bubbled keys, so a future consumer nesting a text field inside `#titles` while `titlesAction` is set would have Space swallowed — invalid ARIA anyway, and the prototype's own handler bubbles identically, so it wants an `e.target !== e.currentTarget` guard only if that composition ever becomes real.

Reviewer independently confirmed all nine files are **MD5-identical** to Google's bytes and every caron glyph is present. ⚠ **A second asset was broken by the same CSP**: `InviteRegister.vue`'s Goriffee logo — and its upstream URL **404s** too, so it had been dead twice over; replaced with Goriffee's current official SVG (**colour treatment differs — design sign-off pending**). New `self-hosted-fonts.spec.js` (9 tests) serves the built app under the **verbatim production CSP** and sweeps `/`, `/invite/:code`, `/g/:token` for any non-same-origin subresource. ## Module 06 closeout — and the end of the effort (2026-08-09, RD-GX-4)

**All 25 rows `[x]`. Modules 02+03, 04, 05 and 06 closed. Suite 525 passed / 3 skipped.**

### The sanctioned-edit ledger — 7 spent, 7 permitted, none beyond

| # | spec item | file | row |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 06 §UC-GX-011 item 1 | `guest-lead-capture.spec.js` `/nabudúce/i` → `/objednať sami/i` | RD-GX-4 |
| 2 | 06 §UC-GX-011 item 2 | `guest-order.spec.js` + `guest-status.spec.js` (two halves) | RD-GX-2 / RD-GX-3 |
| 3–4 | 06 §UC-GX-011 items 3–4 | `guest-order.spec.js` | RD-GX-2 |
| 5 | 06 §UC-GX-011 item 5 | `guest-status.spec.js` | RD-GX-3 |
| 6–7 | 05 §UC-KG-007 item 1 | `guest-link.spec.js`, `guest-admin-view.spec.js` | RD-KG-* |

No `.only`, no added `test.skip`, no deleted or weakened assertion anywhere in the effort.

### What the closeout actually proved

- ⚠ **`git diff --stat 7c3f85e HEAD -- backend/` is EMPTY.** The whole 25-row restyle
  touched no route, no schema, no helper, no middleware. Every GSO-T1..T10 invariant in
  `CLAUDE.md` still holds by construction, not by inspection.
- `frontend/src/api.js` was **never touched by any row** (`git log 7c3f85e..HEAD -- …`
  is empty), so no request shape, header set or endpoint moved.
- `lib/guest-cart.js` changed **exactly once**, in RD-GX-3 (`d08784a`, the `formatPrice`
  deletion with zero importers), and is unchanged since.
- **Admin invariance**, measured against a real build of `7c3f85e` with both servers
  seeded identically at 1280×900: 12 of 14 admin routes **byte-identical**; the 2 that
  differ are data, localised to a random `invite_code` cell (485px) and one digit of a
  port number (71px). ⚠ The run carried a **positive control** — `/` and `/g/:token`
  reported 90.04% and 100% different — without which "identical" is unfalsifiable, and
  a same-server-twice control established a 0-pixel noise floor.

### ⚠ Two false measurement comments, corrected at their call sites

`.m-foot .btn` is `flex:1` (grow 1, basis 0) with the flexbox default `min-width:auto`,
so each button's **min-content is its floor**, and the ≤400px padding relief is
invisible only while the even split already clears **both** floors. That is a narrower
condition than three rows assumed.

- **RD-GX-1's `.gx-foot-btn` claim was FALSE** — "verified: resolved widths unchanged at
  378px" was wrong; the relief moves 12px from the accent button to `Späť` at every
  width the query covers.
- **RD-FO-4's `.fo-foot-btn` claim was FALSE for 1 of its 4 footers** — `Spôsob
  prevzatia` goes 133.95/156.05 → 145/145 at 378px, the same symmetric shape RD-GX-3
  found. The other three genuinely are invisible (both floors already under the split).

Both call sites now carry the measured table rather than the claim. The lesson for any
future footer: ⚠ **`.btn` is `nowrap` + `flex:1`, so a too-wide row gives NO degradation
signal** — no shrink, no wrap, no ellipsis. Measure min-content, never the flex-resolved
width, and never generalise one footer's verdict to another's button pair.

### The gate harness — three ways it lied, all mine

Recorded because every one produced a *plausible* wrong number:

1. **A stale server on the port** silently served a `backend/public` that had been
   deleted and rebuilt underneath it → a false 236/3. Confirm the port is free first.
2. **`| tail` takes tail's exit code**, not the build's — three mutation probes once ran
   against a stale bundle. Always `echo "EXIT: $?"` on its own.
3. ⚠ **`CORS_ORIGIN` must include the gate's own origin.** The backend allowlists
   `gorifi.skolar.sk` / `gorifi-dev.skolar.sk` / `localhost:5173` only, so a same-origin
   SPA on `localhost:3997` gets **500s on every XHR** and renders a blank body — which
   surfaces as 31 unrelated-looking UI failures (admin login, colleagues panel,
   distribution) and reads exactly like a regression. Also: **`e2e/seed.mjs` is not
   optional** on a fresh `DB_PATH`. And ⚠ `pkill -f` matches its own shell (exit 144) —
   chain nothing after it.

### Recorded residuals (none blocking)

- ⚠ **`410 Gone` is browser-cached.** The backend sends no `Cache-Control` on the dead-link
  410, so a reactivated link still shows the dead screen until a hard reload. Pre-existing
  and **backend-owned**, therefore out of scope for a frontend-only effort;
  `guest-invite-dead.spec.js` documents it as the reason each variant gets its own link.
- `api.js:352` still quotes the old fold line "Chcete si nabudúce objednať sami?" in a
  comment. Left stale **deliberately** — `api.js` showing no diff is a closeout condition,
  and a comment cannot mislead a test.
- `GuestOrderStatus.vue`'s dead card also renders for 5xx/network and then advises
  "Skontrolujte, či je odkaz skopírovaný celý", which is wrong for a server error.
  `GuestOrder.vue` got a reason-aware description this row; its twin did not, because
  §UC-GX-010 only specifies the 404 case there. Spec-conformant, worth a follow-up.
- **Spec inaccuracies found**: §UC-GX-009's header ("root keeps `data-testid="invite-cta"`")
  contradicts its own items 3/4, which put `invite-done`/`invite-requested` on the state's
  root — one element cannot hold two testids. Resolved by keeping `invite-cta` on each
  state's root and moving the state testid to the text span (a wrapper was rejected: an
  unclassed block in a gapped flex column is the A10 preflight hazard). Both readings
  satisfy every shipped assertion. Also, §UC-GX-011's "full inventory" omits `cartbar`.
